(* ================================================================= *)
(* HYPER SYNTAX: MATEMATIKAI JELÖLÉSRENDSZER (NOTATIONS)             *)
(* ================================================================= *)

(* 1. Bázis projekciók rövidítései a kényelemért *)
Notation "'π_1'" := b_fst (at level 0).
Notation "'π_2'" := b_snd (at level 0).
Notation "'S'"  := b_S (at level 0).

(* 2. Atomi predikátum alkalmazása (Pullback jelölés: P[t]) *)
(* A 10-es szint biztosítja, hogy ez kössön a legerősebben *)
Notation "P '[' t ']'" := (Ty_Atom P t) (at level 10, no associativity).

(* 3. Logikai Konnektívumok *)
(* Jobbra asszociatívak, a megszokott logikai precedencia-szintekkel *)
Notation "Phi /\ Psi" := (Ty_Prod Phi Psi) (at level 80, right associativity).
Notation "Phi \/ Psi" := (Ty_Sum Phi Psi) (at level 85, right associativity).
Notation "Phi ~> Psi" := (Ty_Arrow Phi Psi) (at level 90, right associativity).
(* Megj: A sima '->' a Coq beépített függvény-nyila, azt ne írjuk felül, használjuk a '~>'-t! *)

(* 4. Kvantorok *)
(* A 200-as szint (leggyengébb kötés) miatt a kvantor az egész utána lévő kifejezésre vonatkozik *)
Notation "'∃' A , Phi" := (Ty_Ex A Phi) (at level 200, right associativity).
Notation "'∀' A , Phi" := (Ty_All A Phi) (at level 200, right associativity).

Variable P : nat. (* pl. 1 *)
Variable Q : nat. (* pl. 2 *)

Definition bal_oldal_szep :=
  ∃ S , (P[π_2] \/ Q[π_2]).

Definition jobb_oldal_szep :=
  (∃ S , P[π_2]) \/ (∃ S , Q[π_2]).

--- FILE: skeleton/Logic/Hyper/Simple/Hyper_Syntax.v ---
(*Logic/Hyper/Simple/Hyper_Syntax.*)

Require Import List.
Import ListNotations.

(* ================================================================= *)
(* MODUL BEEMELÉSE: BÁZIS SZINTAXIS (Individuumok és Kontextusok)    *)
(* ================================================================= *)
Require Import HyperLogic.Logic.Base.Simple.Base_Syntax.
Require Import HyperLogic.Logic.STLC_PI_Sum.STLC_PI_Sum.

(* ================================================================= *)
(* 1. FORMULÁK (HYPER TYPES)                                         *)
(* ================================================================= *)

Inductive h_ty : Type :=
  (* --- A) Propozicionális Modul (A BiCCC / STLC réteg másolata) --- *)
  | Ty_Unit   : h_ty
  | Ty_Void   : h_ty
  | Ty_Prod   : h_ty -> h_ty -> h_ty
  | Ty_Sum    : h_ty -> h_ty -> h_ty
  | Ty_Arrow  : h_ty -> h_ty -> h_ty

  (* --- B) Hyper Modul (Elsőrendű kiegészítések és a Bázis csatolása) --- *)

  (* Predikátum: P(t). A 't' KÖZVETLENÜL a Base_Syntax-ból jön (b_tm)!
     Ez egy t : I -> dom(P) bázis morfizmus. *)
  | Ty_Atom   : nat -> b_tm -> h_ty

  (* Kvantorok: Egy új individuum-típust (A) vezetnek be a Bázisból (b_ty).
     NINCS változónév, csak a kiterjesztett (I x A) bázis-objektum! *)
  | Ty_All    : b_ty -> h_ty -> h_ty
  | Ty_Ex     : b_ty -> h_ty -> h_ty.

Definition h_context := list h_ty.

(* ================================================================= *)
(* 2. BÁZIS HELYETTESÍTÉS (SZINTAKTIKUS PULLBACK)                    *)
(* ================================================================= *)

(* A Base_Syntax b_tm kombinátorait használjuk a helyettesítéshez! *)

(* Ha van egy f : I -> J bázis-helyettesítésünk, és egy kvantor alá lépünk,
   az f-et ki kell terjeszteni I x A -> J x A morfizmussá: f x id_A. *)
Definition b_pullback_ext (f : b_tm) : b_tm :=
  b_pair (b_comp f b_fst) b_snd.

(* A formula visszahúzása (pullback) egy f : I -> J bázis-morfizmus mentén.
   Matematikailag ez a híres f^* (Phi) helyettesítő művelet. *)
Fixpoint form_pullback (f : b_tm) (Phi : h_ty) : h_ty :=
  match Phi with
  (* Propozicionális szint: rekurzív továbbadás *)
  | Ty_Unit => Ty_Unit
  | Ty_Void => Ty_Void
  | Ty_Prod A B => Ty_Prod (form_pullback f A) (form_pullback f B)
  | Ty_Sum A B => Ty_Sum (form_pullback f A) (form_pullback f B)
  | Ty_Arrow A B => Ty_Arrow (form_pullback f A) (form_pullback f B)

  (* Hyper szint (Atomok): A helyettesítés SZÓ SZERINT bázis kompozíció! *)
  | Ty_Atom p t => Ty_Atom p (b_comp t f)

  (* Hyper szint (Kvantorok): A kiterjesztett (f x id) helyettesítés fut! *)
  | Ty_All A Body => Ty_All A (form_pullback (b_pullback_ext f) Body)
  | Ty_Ex A Body => Ty_Ex A (form_pullback (b_pullback_ext f) Body)
  end.

(* Egy teljes logikai kontextus visszahúzása *)
Definition ctx_pullback (f : b_tm) (G : h_context) : h_context :=
  map (form_pullback f) G.

(* ================================================================= *)
(* 3. BIZONYÍTÁSTERMEK (HYPER TERMS)                                 *)
(* ================================================================= *)

Inductive h_tm : Type :=
  (* --- A) Propozicionális Modul --- *)
  | tm_unit   : h_tm
  | tm_var    : nat -> h_tm
  | tm_lam    : h_ty -> h_tm -> h_tm
  | tm_app    : h_tm -> h_tm -> h_tm
  | tm_pair   : h_tm -> h_tm -> h_tm
  | tm_fst    : h_tm -> h_tm
  | tm_snd    : h_tm -> h_tm
  | tm_inl    : h_ty -> h_tm -> h_tm
  | tm_inr    : h_ty -> h_tm -> h_tm
  | tm_case   : h_tm -> h_tm -> h_tm -> h_tm
  | tm_absurd : h_ty -> h_tm -> h_tm

  (* --- B) Hyper Modul (Kvantor Szabályok) --- *)
  | tm_all_intro : h_tm -> h_tm
  (* Az eliminációhoz a Bázisból kérünk egy 'witness' morfizmust (b_tm)! *)
  | tm_all_elim  : h_tm -> b_tm -> h_tm
  | tm_ex_intro  : b_tm -> h_tm -> h_tm
  | tm_ex_elim   : h_tm -> h_tm -> h_tm.

(* ================================================================= *)
(* 4. DUAL CONTEXT TYPING (Bizonyíthatóság)                          *)
(* ================================================================= *)

(* Jelölés: I | Gamma |- p : Phi
   I: b_ty (A Base kontextus, ahol az individuumok élnek)
   G: h_context (A logikai kontextus) *)
Inductive h_typing (I : b_ty) : h_context -> h_tm -> h_ty -> Prop :=

  (* --- Strukturális Szabályok --- *)
  | HT_Var0 : forall G Phi,
      h_typing I (Phi :: G) (tm_var 0) Phi

  | HT_VarS : forall G Phi Psi i,
      h_typing I G (tm_var i) Phi ->
      h_typing I (Psi :: G) (tm_var (S i)) Phi

  (* --- Propozicionális Modul (Az 'I' bázis passzív marad) --- *)
  | HT_Unit : forall G,
      h_typing I G tm_unit Ty_Unit

  | HT_Lam : forall G p Phi Psi,
      h_typing I (Phi :: G) p Psi ->
      h_typing I G (tm_lam Phi p) (Ty_Arrow Phi Psi)

  | HT_App : forall G p1 p2 Phi Psi,
      h_typing I G p1 (Ty_Arrow Phi Psi) ->
      h_typing I G p2 Phi ->
      h_typing I G (tm_app p1 p2) Psi

  | HT_Pair : forall G p1 p2 Phi Psi,
      h_typing I G p1 Phi ->
      h_typing I G p2 Psi ->
      h_typing I G (tm_pair p1 p2) (Ty_Prod Phi Psi)

  | HT_Fst : forall G p Phi Psi,
      h_typing I G p (Ty_Prod Phi Psi) ->
      h_typing I G (tm_fst p) Phi

  | HT_Snd : forall G p Phi Psi,
      h_typing I G p (Ty_Prod Phi Psi) ->
      h_typing I G (tm_snd p) Psi

  | HT_Inl : forall G p Phi Psi,
      h_typing I G p Phi ->
      h_typing I G (tm_inl Psi p) (Ty_Sum Phi Psi)

  | HT_Inr : forall G p Phi Psi,
      h_typing I G p Psi ->
      h_typing I G (tm_inr Phi p) (Ty_Sum Phi Psi)

  | HT_Case : forall G p p1 p2 Phi Psi Chi,
      h_typing I G p (Ty_Sum Phi Psi) ->
      h_typing I (Phi :: G) p1 Chi ->
      h_typing I (Psi :: G) p2 Chi ->
      h_typing I G (tm_case p p1 p2) Chi

  | HT_Absurd : forall G p Phi,
      h_typing I G p Ty_Void ->
      h_typing I G (tm_absurd Phi p) Phi

  (* --- Hyper Modul (Dual Context interakció a Bázissal!) --- *)

  (* ALL Intro: A bázist megnöveljük (I x A)-ra.
     A kontextust visszahúzzuk a pi_1 mentén (Weakening) *)
  | HT_All_Intro : forall G p A Phi,
      h_typing (b_Prod I A) (ctx_pullback b_fst G) p Phi ->
      h_typing I G (tm_all_intro p) (Ty_All A Phi)

  (* ALL Elim: f : I -> A bázis-morfizmussal (b_typing) specializálunk.
     A form_pullback <id_I, f> húzza vissza a formulát I-re! *)
  | HT_All_Elim : forall G p A Phi f,
      h_typing I G p (Ty_All A Phi) ->
      base_typing I f A ->
      h_typing I G (tm_all_elim p f) (form_pullback (b_pair b_id f) Phi)

  (* EX Intro: f : I -> A bázis-morfizmussal adunk egy tanút. *)
  | HT_Ex_Intro : forall G p A Phi f,
      base_typing I f A ->
      h_typing I G p (form_pullback (b_pair b_id f) Phi) ->
      h_typing I G (tm_ex_intro f p) (Ty_Ex A Phi)

  (* EX Elim: Dual Context csere a bal adjungálthoz. *)
  | HT_Ex_Elim : forall G p p2 A Phi Chi,
      h_typing I G p (Ty_Ex A Phi) ->
      h_typing (b_Prod I A) (Phi :: ctx_pullback b_fst G) p2 (form_pullback b_fst Chi) ->
      h_typing I G (tm_ex_elim p p2) Chi.

(* ================================================================= *)
(* 4.5 DERIVATION TREES (DATA LAYER - Set)                           *)
(* ================================================================= *)

Inductive h_derivation (I : b_ty) : h_context -> h_tm -> h_ty -> Set :=

  (* --- Strukturális Szabályok --- *)
  | HD_Var0 : forall G Phi,
      h_derivation I (Phi :: G) (tm_var 0) Phi
  | HD_VarS : forall G Phi Psi i,
      h_derivation I G (tm_var i) Phi ->
      h_derivation I (Psi :: G) (tm_var (S i)) Phi

  (* --- Propozicionális Modul --- *)
  | HD_Unit : forall G, h_derivation I G tm_unit Ty_Unit
  | HD_Lam : forall G p Phi Psi,
      h_derivation I (Phi :: G) p Psi ->
      h_derivation I G (tm_lam Phi p) (Ty_Arrow Phi Psi)
  | HD_App : forall G p1 p2 Phi Psi,
      h_derivation I G p1 (Ty_Arrow Phi Psi) ->
      h_derivation I G p2 Phi ->
      h_derivation I G (tm_app p1 p2) Psi
  | HD_Pair : forall G p1 p2 Phi Psi,
      h_derivation I G p1 Phi ->
      h_derivation I G p2 Psi ->
      h_derivation I G (tm_pair p1 p2) (Ty_Prod Phi Psi)
  | HD_Fst : forall G p Phi Psi,
      h_derivation I G p (Ty_Prod Phi Psi) ->
      h_derivation I G (tm_fst p) Phi
  | HD_Snd : forall G p Phi Psi,
      h_derivation I G p (Ty_Prod Phi Psi) ->
      h_derivation I G (tm_snd p) Psi
  | HD_Inl : forall G p Phi Psi,
      h_derivation I G p Phi ->
      h_derivation I G (tm_inl Psi p) (Ty_Sum Phi Psi)
  | HD_Inr : forall G p Phi Psi,
      h_derivation I G p Psi ->
      h_derivation I G (tm_inr Phi p) (Ty_Sum Phi Psi)
  | HD_Case : forall G p p1 p2 Phi Psi Chi,
      h_derivation I G p (Ty_Sum Phi Psi) ->
      h_derivation I (Phi :: G) p1 Chi ->
      h_derivation I (Psi :: G) p2 Chi ->
      h_derivation I G (tm_case p p1 p2) Chi
  | HD_Absurd : forall G p Phi,
      h_derivation I G p Ty_Void ->
      h_derivation I G (tm_absurd Phi p) Phi

  (* --- Hyper Modul --- *)
  | HD_All_Intro : forall G p A Phi,
      h_derivation (b_Prod I A) (ctx_pullback b_fst G) p Phi ->
      h_derivation I G (tm_all_intro p) (Ty_All A Phi)
  | HD_All_Elim : forall G p A Phi f,
      h_derivation I G p (Ty_All A Phi) ->
      base_typing I f A ->
      h_derivation I G (tm_all_elim p f) (form_pullback (b_pair b_id f) Phi)
  | HD_Ex_Intro : forall G p A Phi f,
      base_typing I f A ->
      h_derivation I G p (form_pullback (b_pair b_id f) Phi) ->
      h_derivation I G (tm_ex_intro f p) (Ty_Ex A Phi)
  | HD_Ex_Elim : forall G p p2 A Phi Chi,
      h_derivation I G p (Ty_Ex A Phi) ->
      h_derivation (b_Prod I A) (Phi :: ctx_pullback b_fst G) p2 (form_pullback b_fst Chi) ->
      h_derivation I G (tm_ex_elim p p2) Chi.

(* Bridge a Set és Prop között
Fixpoint forget_h_derivation {I G p Phi} (D : h_derivation I G p Phi) : h_typing I G p Phi.
Proof. Admitted. *)

(* ================================================================= *)
(* 5. PROOF IRRELEVANCE EKVIVALENCIA                                 *)
(* ================================================================= *)

(* Ez mondja ki, hogy a szálak Thin kategóriák: minden, ami azonos
   típussal (Phi) bizonyítható, az triviálisan ekvivalens! *)
Inductive h_equiv_rel (I : b_ty) : h_tm -> h_tm -> Prop :=
  | PI_rule : forall G Phi p q,
      h_typing I G p Phi ->
      h_typing I G q Phi ->
      h_equiv_rel I p q.

(* ================================================================= *)
(*          TÍPUSOK (FORMULÁK) BEÁGYAZÁSA (EMBEDDING)                *)
(* ================================================================= *)

Fixpoint embed_ty (A : STLC_PI_Sum.ty) : h_ty :=
  match A with
  (* Az STLC atomi kijelentéseit 0-változós predikátumként ágyazzuk be,
     amit a bázis 'b_unit' (üres/triviális) termjére alkalmazunk! *)
  | STLC_PI_Sum.Ty_Atomic p  => Ty_Atom p b_unit
  | STLC_PI_Sum.Ty_Unit      => Ty_Unit
  | STLC_PI_Sum.Ty_Void      => Ty_Void
  | STLC_PI_Sum.Ty_Arrow A B => Ty_Arrow (embed_ty A) (embed_ty B)
  | STLC_PI_Sum.Ty_Prod A B  => Ty_Prod (embed_ty A) (embed_ty B)
  | STLC_PI_Sum.Ty_Sum A B   => Ty_Sum (embed_ty A) (embed_ty B)
  end.

(* Egy teljes logikai kontextus lefordítása *)
Definition embed_ctx (G : STLC_PI_Sum.context) : h_context :=
  map embed_ty G.

(* ================================================================= *)
(* 2. BIZONYÍTÁSTERMEK BEÁGYAZÁSA                                    *)
(* ================================================================= *)

Fixpoint embed_tm (t : STLC_PI_Sum.tm) : h_tm :=
  match t with
  | STLC_PI_Sum.tm_unit      => tm_unit
  | STLC_PI_Sum.tm_var i     => tm_var i
  | STLC_PI_Sum.tm_lam A t'  => tm_lam (embed_ty A) (embed_tm t')
  | STLC_PI_Sum.tm_app t1 t2 => tm_app (embed_tm t1) (embed_tm t2)
  | STLC_PI_Sum.tm_pair t1 t2=> tm_pair (embed_tm t1) (embed_tm t2)
  | STLC_PI_Sum.tm_fst t'    => tm_fst (embed_tm t')
  | STLC_PI_Sum.tm_snd t'    => tm_snd (embed_tm t')
  | STLC_PI_Sum.tm_inl B t'  => tm_inl (embed_ty B) (embed_tm t')
  | STLC_PI_Sum.tm_inr A t'  => tm_inr (embed_ty A) (embed_tm t')
  | STLC_PI_Sum.tm_case t1 t2 t3 => tm_case (embed_tm t1) (embed_tm t2) (embed_tm t3)
  | STLC_PI_Sum.tm_absurd A t' => tm_absurd (embed_ty A) (embed_tm t')
  end.

(* ================================================================= *)
(* 3. LIFTING THEOREM (A BIZONYÍTÁSOK ÁTMENTÉSE)                     *)
(* ================================================================= *)

(** Ez a tétel mondja ki: ami bizonyítható a propozicionális STLC-ben,
    az automatikusan bizonyítható a Hyper logikában (bármely I bázis felett)! *)
Theorem embed_typing_sound : forall (G : STLC_PI_Sum.context) (t : STLC_PI_Sum.tm) (A : STLC_PI_Sum.ty) (I : b_ty),
  STLC_PI_Sum.typeability G t A ->
  h_typing I (embed_ctx G) (embed_tm t) (embed_ty A).
Proof. Admitted.--- FILE: skeleton/Logic/Hyper/Simple/Hyper_Logic.v ---
Require Import List.
Require Import PeanoNat.
Import ListNotations.

Require Import HyperLogic.Logic.Base.Simple.Base_Syntax.
Require Import HyperLogic.Logic.Base.Simple.Base_Typing.
Require Import HyperLogic.Logic.Base.Simple.Base_Equiv.
Require Import HyperLogic.Logic.Hyper.Simple.Hyper_Syntax.
Require Import HyperLogic.Logic.Hyper.Simple.Hyper_Substitution.
Require Import HyperLogic.Logic.Hyper.Simple.Hyper_Typing.

(* Induktív reláció: entails G Phi Psi
   Jelölés: G | Phi |- Psi

   Jelentése: A 'G' bázis-kontextusban (ahol a változók élnek),
   ha a 'Phi' formula igaz, akkor a 'Psi' formula is igaz.

   Ez felel meg a Fiber kategória morfizmusainak.
*)

Inductive entails (G : b_context) : h_ty -> h_ty -> Prop :=

  (* --- 1. Structural Rules (Identity & Cut) --- *)

  | L_Id : forall A,
      formula_wf G A -> (* Csak jól formált formulákon *)
      entails G A A

  | L_Comp : forall A B C,
      entails G A B ->
      entails G B C ->
      entails G A C

  (* --- 2. Propositional Logic (BiCCC Rules) --- *)

  (* TRUE (Top) *)
  | L_Top_Intro : forall A,
      formula_wf G A ->
      entails G A Ty_Unit

  (* FALSE (Bottom) *)
  | L_Bot_Elim : forall A,
      formula_wf G A ->
      entails G Ty_Void A

  (* AND (Product) *)
  | L_And_Intro : forall A B C,
      entails G C A ->
      entails G C B ->
      entails G C (Ty_Prod A B)

  | L_And_E1 : forall A B,
      formula_wf G B ->
      entails G (Ty_Prod A B) A

  | L_And_E2 : forall A B,
      formula_wf G A ->
      entails G (Ty_Prod A B) B

  (* OR (Sum) *)
  | L_Or_Intro1 : forall A B,
      formula_wf G B ->
      entails G A (Ty_Sum A B)

  | L_Or_Intro2 : forall A B,
      formula_wf G A ->
      entails G B (Ty_Sum A B)

  | L_Or_Elim : forall A B C,
      entails G A C ->
      entails G B C ->
      entails G (Ty_Sum A B) C

  (* IMPLICATION (Exponential) *)
  (* Adjungálás: (C /\ A -> B) <==> (C -> (A => B)) *)

  | L_Arr_Intro : forall A B C,
      entails G (Ty_Prod C A) B ->  (* Curry *)
      entails G C (Ty_Arr A B)

  | L_Arr_Elim : forall A B,
      formula_wf G (Ty_Arr A B) ->
      entails G (Ty_Prod (Ty_Arr A B) A) B (* Eval *)

  (* --- 3. Quantifiers (The Typeability Flavor!) --- *)

  (* FORALL (Universal Quantifier) *)
  (* Jobb adjungáltja a Weakening-nek (Shift) *)

  | L_All_Intro : forall A Phi Psi,
      (* Ha kiterjesztett kontextusban (A::G) a 'Psi' (eltolva) implikálja 'Phi'-t... *)
      entails (A :: G) (h_shift 1 0 Psi) Phi ->
      (* ...akkor az alap kontextusban 'Psi' implikálja 'Minden A. Phi'-t *)
      entails G Psi (Ty_All A Phi)

  | L_All_Elim : forall A Phi t,
      (* Typeability Check! 't' legyen érvényes term 'A' típussal G-ben *)
      base_typing G t A ->
      (* All A. Phi |- Phi[t/0] *)
      entails G (Ty_All A Phi) (h_subst 0 t Phi)

  (* EXISTS (Existential Quantifier) *)
  (* Bal adjungáltja a Weakening-nek (Shift) *)

  | L_Ex_Intro : forall A Phi t,
      (* Typeability Check! *)
      base_typing G t A ->
      (* Phi[t/0] |- Ex A. Phi *)
      entails G (h_subst 0 t Phi) (Ty_Ex A Phi)

  | L_Ex_Elim : forall A Phi Psi,
      (* Ha kiterjesztett kontextusban 'Phi' implikálja 'Psi'-t (eltolva)... *)
      entails (A :: G) Phi (h_shift 1 0 Psi) ->
      (* ...akkor 'Ex A. Phi' implikálja 'Psi'-t *)
      entails G (Ty_Ex A Phi) Psi

  (* --- 4. Conversion Rule (Interaction with Base Equality) --- *)

  (* Ha két term egyenlő a Base-ben (pl. beta redukció),
     akkor a rájuk épülő atomi állítások is ekvivalensek. *)
  | L_Cast : forall p ts1 ts2,
      (* Ha a termlisták elemenként egyenlőek... (ezt definiálni kellene, de most egyszerűsítünk) *)
      (* Itt egy egyszerűsített szabály: ha a szintaxisban csere történik *)
      ts1 = ts2 -> (* Ez placeholder a base_equiv listára *)
      entails G (Ty_Atom p ts1) (Ty_Atom p ts2).--- FILE: skeleton/Logic/Hyper/Epsilon_Adjoint/Hyper_Syntax_Lemmas.v ---
(* theories/Logic/Hyper/Epsilon_Adjoint/Hyper_Syntax_Lemmas.v *)

Require Import List.
Import ListNotations.
Require Import HyperLogic.Logic.Base.Epsilon_Adjoint.Base_Syntax.
Require Import HyperLogic.Logic.STLC_PI_Sum.STLC_PI_Sum.
Require Import HyperLogic.Logic.Hyper.Epsilon_Adjoint.Hyper_Syntax.

Lemma phi_iso_phi_id_snd : forall {Sign : Type} `{SkolemMapping Sign} (I A : b_ty) (Phi : @h_ty Sign)
  (wf : WellFormed (b_Prod I A) Phi),
  form_equiv (b_Prod I A) Phi (form_subst (b_pair b_id b_snd) (form_subst b_fst Phi)).
Proof. Admitted. (* A Qed-hez már csak a bázis egyenlőség admit-ját kell feloldani *)

Lemma eps_implies_ex : forall {Sign : Type} `{SkolemMapping Sign} (I A : b_ty) (Phi : @h_ty Sign),
  h_derivation I [ Phi .[ ε A, Phi ] ]
    (tm_ex_intro (b_const A (skolem_index A Phi)) (tm_var 0))
    (Ty_Ex A Phi).
Proof. Admitted.

Lemma ex_implies_eps : forall {Sign : Type} `{SkolemMapping Sign} (I A : b_ty) (Phi : @h_ty Sign),
  h_derivation I [ Ty_Ex A Phi ]
    (tm_ex_elim (tm_var 0) (tm_eps_intro b_snd (tm_var 0)))
    (Phi .[ ε A, Phi ]).
Proof. Admitted.

--- FILE: skeleton/Logic/Hyper/Epsilon_Adjoint/Hyper_Syntax.v ---
(* theories/Logic/Hyper/Epsilon_Adjoint/Hyper_Syntax.v *)

Require Import List.
Import ListNotations.
Require Import HyperLogic.Logic.Base.Epsilon_Adjoint.Base_Syntax.
Require Import HyperLogic.Logic.STLC_PI_Sum.STLC_PI_Sum.

(* ================================================================= *)
(* 1. FORMULÁK                                                       *)
(* ================================================================= *)

Inductive h_ty {Sign : Type} : Type :=
  | Ty_Unit   : h_ty
  | Ty_Void   : h_ty
  | Ty_Prod   : h_ty -> h_ty -> h_ty
  | Ty_Sum    : h_ty -> h_ty -> h_ty
  | Ty_Arrow  : h_ty -> h_ty -> h_ty
  | Ty_Atom   : nat -> @b_tm Sign -> h_ty
  | Ty_All    : b_ty -> h_ty -> h_ty
  | Ty_Ex     : b_ty -> h_ty -> h_ty.

Definition h_context {Sign : Type} := list (@h_ty Sign).

(* ================================================================= *)
(* 1.5 AZ ORÁKULUM OSZTÁLY                                           *)
(* ================================================================= *)

Class SkolemMapping (Sign : Type) := {
  skolem_index : forall (A : b_ty), @h_ty Sign -> Sign ;
  skolem_inj : forall A B Phi Psi,
                 skolem_index A Phi = skolem_index B Psi ->
                 A = B /\ Phi = Psi
}.

(* ================================================================= *)
(* 2. BIZONYÍTÁSTERMEK (Explicit Epszilon termekkel)                 *)
(* ================================================================= *)

Inductive h_tm {Sign : Type} : Type :=
  | tm_unit   : h_tm
  | tm_var    : nat -> h_tm
  | tm_lam    : @h_ty Sign -> h_tm -> h_tm
  | tm_app    : h_tm -> h_tm -> h_tm
  | tm_pair   : h_tm -> h_tm -> h_tm
  | tm_fst    : h_tm -> h_tm
  | tm_snd    : h_tm -> h_tm
  | tm_inl    : @h_ty Sign -> h_tm -> h_tm
  | tm_inr    : @h_ty Sign -> h_tm -> h_tm
  | tm_case   : h_tm -> h_tm -> h_tm -> h_tm
  | tm_absurd : @h_ty Sign -> h_tm -> h_tm
  | tm_all_intro : h_tm -> h_tm
  | tm_all_elim  : h_tm -> @b_tm Sign -> h_tm
  | tm_ex_intro  : @b_tm Sign -> h_tm -> h_tm
  | tm_ex_elim   : h_tm -> h_tm -> h_tm
  | tm_eps_intro : @b_tm Sign -> h_tm -> h_tm
  | tm_eps_elim  : h_tm -> h_tm -> h_tm.

(* ================================================================= *)
(* BÁZIS HELYETTESÍTÉS ÉS PULLBACK                                   *)
(* ================================================================= *)

Definition b_subst_ext {Sign : Type} (f : @b_tm Sign) : @b_tm Sign :=
  b_pair (b_comp f b_fst) b_snd.

Fixpoint form_subst {Sign : Type} (f : @b_tm Sign) (Phi : @h_ty Sign) : @h_ty Sign :=
  match Phi with
  | Ty_Unit => Ty_Unit
  | Ty_Void => Ty_Void
  | Ty_Prod A B => Ty_Prod (form_subst f A) (form_subst f B)
  | Ty_Sum A B => Ty_Sum (form_subst f A) (form_subst f B)
  | Ty_Arrow A B => Ty_Arrow (form_subst f A) (form_subst f B)
  | Ty_Atom p t => Ty_Atom p (b_comp t f)
  | Ty_All A Body => Ty_All A (form_subst (b_subst_ext f) Body)
  | Ty_Ex A Body => Ty_Ex A (form_subst (b_subst_ext f) Body)
  end.

Definition ctx_subst {Sign : Type} (f : @b_tm Sign) (G : @h_context Sign) : @h_context Sign :=
  map (form_subst f) G.

Declare Scope surface_scope.
Notation "Phi .[ t ]" := (form_subst (b_pair b_id t) Phi) (at level 40, left associativity) : surface_scope.
Notation "'ε' D , Phi" := (b_const D (skolem_index D Phi)) (at level 50) : surface_scope.
Open Scope surface_scope.

(* ================================================================= *)
(* WELLFORMED FORMULAS (WFF)                                         *)
(* ================================================================= *)

Inductive WellFormed {Sign : Type} (I : b_ty) : @h_ty Sign -> Type :=
  | WF_Unit   : WellFormed I Ty_Unit
  | WF_Void   : WellFormed I Ty_Void
  | WF_Prod   : forall Phi Psi, WellFormed I Phi -> WellFormed I Psi -> WellFormed I (Ty_Prod Phi Psi)
  | WF_Sum    : forall Phi Psi, WellFormed I Phi -> WellFormed I Psi -> WellFormed I (Ty_Sum Phi Psi)
  | WF_Arrow  : forall Phi Psi, WellFormed I Phi -> WellFormed I Psi -> WellFormed I (Ty_Arrow Phi Psi)
  | WF_Atom   : forall p t A, base_typing I t A -> WellFormed I (Ty_Atom p t)
  | WF_All    : forall A Phi, WellFormed (b_Prod I A) Phi -> WellFormed I (Ty_All A Phi)
  | WF_Ex     : forall A Phi, WellFormed (b_Prod I A) Phi -> WellFormed I (Ty_Ex A Phi).

(* ================================================================= *)
(* 2.5 FORMULA EKVIVALENCIA (Az Adjungált Modellhez)                 *)
(* ================================================================= *)

Inductive form_equiv {Sign : Type} `{SkolemMapping Sign} (I : b_ty) : @h_ty Sign -> @h_ty Sign -> Type :=

  | FE_Atom : forall p (t1 t2 : @b_tm Sign) A (d1 : @base_typing Sign I t1 A) (d2 : @base_typing Sign I t2 A),
      @base_equiv Sign I A t1 t2 d1 d2 -> form_equiv I (Ty_Atom p t1) (Ty_Atom p t2)
  | FE_Refl : forall Phi, WellFormed I Phi -> form_equiv I Phi Phi
  | FE_Sym  : forall Phi Psi, form_equiv I Phi Psi -> form_equiv I Psi Phi
  | FE_Trans: forall Phi Psi Chi, form_equiv I Phi Psi -> form_equiv I Psi Chi -> form_equiv I Phi Chi
  | FE_Prod : forall A B A' B', form_equiv I A A' -> form_equiv I B B' -> form_equiv I (Ty_Prod A B) (Ty_Prod A' B')
  | FE_Sum  : forall A B A' B', form_equiv I A A' -> form_equiv I B B' -> form_equiv I (Ty_Sum A B) (Ty_Sum A' B')
  | FE_Arrow: forall A B A' B', form_equiv I A A' -> form_equiv I B B' -> form_equiv I (Ty_Arrow A B) (Ty_Arrow A' B')
  | FE_All  : forall D Body Body', form_equiv (b_Prod I D) Body Body' -> form_equiv I (Ty_All D Body) (Ty_All D Body')
  | FE_Ex   : forall D Body Body', form_equiv (b_Prod I D) Body Body' -> form_equiv I (Ty_Ex D Body) (Ty_Ex D Body')

  (* --- ÚJ: ADJUNGÁLT EPSZILON AXIÓMÁK --- *)
  | FE_Eps_Adjoint_Iso : forall A Phi,
      WellFormed (b_Prod I A) Phi ->
      form_equiv I (Ty_Ex A Phi) (Phi .[ ε A, Phi ])

  | FE_Eps_Functorial : forall A Phi Psi,
      form_equiv (b_Prod I A) Phi Psi ->
      form_equiv I (Phi .[ ε A, Phi ]) (Psi .[ ε A, Psi ])

  (* --- FIBRÁCIÓS FUNKTOR SZABÁLYOK --- *)
  | FE_Pullback_Id : forall Phi, WellFormed I Phi -> form_equiv I (form_subst b_id Phi) Phi
  | FE_Pullback_Comp : forall J K (f g : @b_tm Sign) Phi (df : base_typing J f K) (dg : base_typing I g J),
      WellFormed K Phi -> form_equiv I (form_subst g (form_subst f Phi)) (form_subst (b_comp f g) Phi)
  | FE_Pullback_Cong : forall J (f g : @b_tm Sign) Phi (df : base_typing I f J) (dg : base_typing I g J),
      @base_equiv Sign I J f g df dg -> WellFormed J Phi -> form_equiv I (form_subst f Phi) (form_subst g Phi).

(* ================================================================= *)
(* 2.6 KONTEXTUS EKVIVALENCIA (A Completenesshez)                    *)
(* ================================================================= *)

Inductive ctx_equiv {Sign : Type} `{SkolemMapping Sign} (I : b_ty) : @h_context Sign -> @h_context Sign -> Type :=
  | CEq_Nil  : ctx_equiv I [] []
  | CEq_Cons : forall Phi Psi G1 G2,
                 form_equiv I Phi Psi ->
                 ctx_equiv I G1 G2 ->
                 ctx_equiv I (Phi :: G1) (Psi :: G2).

(* ================================================================= *)
(* 3. DUAL CONTEXT TYPING                                            *)
(* ================================================================= *)

Inductive h_typing {Sign : Type} `{SkolemMapping Sign} (I : b_ty) : @h_context Sign -> @h_tm Sign -> @h_ty Sign -> Prop :=
  | HT_Var0 : forall G Phi, h_typing I (Phi :: G) (tm_var 0) Phi
  | HT_VarS : forall G Phi Psi i, h_typing I G (tm_var i) Phi -> h_typing I (Psi :: G) (tm_var (S i)) Phi

  | HT_Conv : forall G p Phi Psi, h_typing I G p Phi -> form_equiv I Phi Psi -> h_typing I G p Psi
  | HT_Ctx_Conv : forall G1 G2 p Phi, ctx_equiv I G1 G2 -> h_typing I G1 p Phi -> h_typing I G2 p Phi

  | HT_Unit : forall G, h_typing I G tm_unit Ty_Unit
  | HT_Lam : forall G p Phi Psi, h_typing I (Phi :: G) p Psi -> h_typing I G (tm_lam Phi p) (Ty_Arrow Phi Psi)
  | HT_App : forall G p1 p2 Phi Psi, h_typing I G p1 (Ty_Arrow Phi Psi) -> h_typing I G p2 Phi -> h_typing I G (tm_app p1 p2) Psi
  | HT_Pair : forall G p1 p2 Phi Psi, h_typing I G p1 Phi -> h_typing I G p2 Psi -> h_typing I G (tm_pair p1 p2) (Ty_Prod Phi Psi)
  | HT_Fst : forall G p Phi Psi, h_typing I G p (Ty_Prod Phi Psi) -> h_typing I G (tm_fst p) Phi
  | HT_Snd : forall G p Phi Psi, h_typing I G p (Ty_Prod Phi Psi) -> h_typing I G (tm_snd p) Psi
  | HT_Inl : forall G p Phi Psi, h_typing I G p Phi -> h_typing I G (tm_inl Psi p) (Ty_Sum Phi Psi)
  | HT_Inr : forall G p Phi Psi, h_typing I G p Psi -> h_typing I G (tm_inr Phi p) (Ty_Sum Phi Psi)
  | HT_Case : forall G p p1 p2 Phi Psi Chi, h_typing I G p (Ty_Sum Phi Psi) -> h_typing I (Phi :: G) p1 Chi -> h_typing I (Psi :: G) p2 Chi -> h_typing I G (tm_case p p1 p2) Chi
  | HT_Absurd : forall G p Phi, h_typing I G p Ty_Void -> h_typing I G (tm_absurd Phi p) Phi

  | HT_All_Intro : forall G p A Phi, h_typing (b_Prod I A) (ctx_subst b_fst G) p Phi -> h_typing I G (tm_all_intro p) (Ty_All A Phi)
  | HT_All_Elim : forall G p A Phi f, h_typing I G p (Ty_All A Phi) -> base_typing I f A -> h_typing I G (tm_all_elim p f) (form_subst (b_pair b_id f) Phi)

  | HT_Ex_Intro : forall G p A Phi f, base_typing I f A -> h_typing I G p (form_subst (b_pair b_id f) Phi) -> h_typing I G (tm_ex_intro f p) (Ty_Ex A Phi)
  | HT_Ex_Elim : forall G p p2 A Phi Chi, h_typing I G p (Ty_Ex A Phi) -> h_typing (b_Prod I A) (Phi :: ctx_subst b_fst G) p2 (form_subst b_fst Chi) -> h_typing I G (tm_ex_elim p p2) Chi

  (* --- INTENZIONÁLIS EPSZILON SZABÁLYOK --- *)
  | HT_Eps_Intro : forall G p A Phi f,
      base_typing I f A ->
      h_typing I G p (form_subst (b_pair b_id f) Phi) ->
      h_typing I G (tm_eps_intro f p)
                   (form_subst (b_pair b_id (b_const A (skolem_index A Phi))) Phi)

  | HT_Eps_Elim : forall G p p2 A Phi Chi,
      h_typing I G p (form_subst (b_pair b_id (b_const A (skolem_index A Phi))) Phi) ->
      h_typing (b_Prod I A) (Phi :: ctx_subst b_fst G) p2 (form_subst b_fst Chi) ->
      h_typing I G (tm_eps_elim p p2) Chi.

(* ================================================================= *)
(* 4. DUAL CONTEXT DERIVATION (Set-alapú)                            *)
(* ================================================================= *)

Inductive h_derivation {Sign : Type} `{SkolemMapping Sign} (I : b_ty) : @h_context Sign -> @h_tm Sign -> @h_ty Sign -> Type :=
  | HD_Var0 : forall G Phi, h_derivation I (Phi :: G) (tm_var 0) Phi
  | HD_VarS : forall G Phi Psi i, h_derivation I G (tm_var i) Phi -> h_derivation I (Psi :: G) (tm_var (S i)) Phi

  | HD_Conv : forall G p Phi Psi, h_derivation I G p Phi -> form_equiv I Phi Psi -> h_derivation I G p Psi
  | HD_Ctx_Conv : forall G1 G2 p Phi, ctx_equiv I G1 G2 -> h_derivation I G1 p Phi -> h_derivation I G2 p Phi

  | HD_Unit : forall G, h_derivation I G tm_unit Ty_Unit
  | HD_Lam : forall G p Phi Psi, h_derivation I (Phi :: G) p Psi -> h_derivation I G (tm_lam Phi p) (Ty_Arrow Phi Psi)
  | HD_App : forall G p1 p2 Phi Psi, h_derivation I G p1 (Ty_Arrow Phi Psi) -> h_derivation I G p2 Phi -> h_derivation I G (tm_app p1 p2) Psi
  | HD_Pair : forall G p1 p2 Phi Psi, h_derivation I G p1 Phi -> h_derivation I G p2 Psi -> h_derivation I G (tm_pair p1 p2) (Ty_Prod Phi Psi)
  | HD_Fst : forall G p Phi Psi, h_derivation I G p (Ty_Prod Phi Psi) -> h_derivation I G (tm_fst p) Phi
  | HD_Snd : forall G p Phi Psi, h_derivation I G p (Ty_Prod Phi Psi) -> h_derivation I G (tm_snd p) Psi
  | HD_Inl : forall G p Phi Psi, h_derivation I G p Phi -> h_derivation I G (tm_inl Psi p) (Ty_Sum Phi Psi)
  | HD_Inr : forall G p Phi Psi, h_derivation I G p Psi -> h_derivation I G (tm_inr Phi p) (Ty_Sum Phi Psi)
  | HD_Case : forall G p p1 p2 Phi Psi Chi, h_derivation I G p (Ty_Sum Phi Psi) -> h_derivation I (Phi :: G) p1 Chi -> h_derivation I (Psi :: G) p2 Chi -> h_derivation I G (tm_case p p1 p2) Chi
  | HD_Absurd : forall G p Phi, h_derivation I G p Ty_Void -> h_derivation I G (tm_absurd Phi p) Phi

  | HD_All_Intro : forall G p A Phi, h_derivation (b_Prod I A) (ctx_subst b_fst G) p Phi -> h_derivation I G (tm_all_intro p) (Ty_All A Phi)
  | HD_All_Elim : forall G p A Phi f, h_derivation I G p (Ty_All A Phi) -> base_typing I f A -> h_derivation I G (tm_all_elim p f) (form_subst (b_pair b_id f) Phi)

  | HD_Ex_Intro : forall G p A Phi f, base_typing I f A -> h_derivation I G p (form_subst (b_pair b_id f) Phi) -> h_derivation I G (tm_ex_intro f p) (Ty_Ex A Phi)
  | HD_Ex_Elim : forall G p p2 A Phi Chi, h_derivation I G p (Ty_Ex A Phi) -> h_derivation (b_Prod I A) (Phi :: ctx_subst b_fst G) p2 (form_subst b_fst Chi) -> h_derivation I G (tm_ex_elim p p2) Chi

  | HD_Eps_Intro : forall G p A Phi f,
      base_typing I f A ->
      h_derivation I G p (form_subst (b_pair b_id f) Phi) ->
      h_derivation I G (tm_eps_intro f p)
                   (form_subst (b_pair b_id (b_const A (skolem_index A Phi))) Phi)

  | HD_Eps_Elim : forall G p p2 A Phi Chi,
      h_derivation I G p (form_subst (b_pair b_id (b_const A (skolem_index A Phi))) Phi) ->
      h_derivation (b_Prod I A) (Phi :: ctx_subst b_fst G) p2 (form_subst b_fst Chi) ->
      h_derivation I G (tm_eps_elim p p2) Chi.
--- FILE: skeleton/Logic/STLC_PI/STLC_PI_Lemmas.v ---
Require Import List Arith PeanoNat Lia.
Import ListNotations.
Require Import HyperLogic.Logic.STLC_PI.STLC_PI.

Open Scope stlc_scope.

(* ================================================================= *)
(* 1. SYNTAX OPERATIONS                                              *)
(* ================================================================= *)

(**
 * Operation: lift
 * Description: Shifts De Bruijn indices to handle variable binding.
 * Usage: `lift n k t` adds `n` to all free variables in `t` (indices >= `k`).
 *)
Fixpoint lift (n : nat) (k : nat) (t : tm) : tm :=
  match t with
  | tm_unit => tm_unit
  | tm_var i =>
      if k <=? i then tm_var (n + i) else tm_var i
  | tm_lam A t' =>
      tm_lam A (lift n (S k) t')
  | tm_app t1 t2 =>
      tm_app (lift n k t1) (lift n k t2)
  | tm_pair t1 t2 =>
      tm_pair (lift n k t1) (lift n k t2)
  | tm_fst t' =>
      tm_fst (lift n k t')
  | tm_snd t' =>
      tm_snd (lift n k t')
  end.

(**
 * Operation: insert_at
 * Description: Inserts a type `B` into context `Gamma` at position `n`.
 *)
Fixpoint insert_at (n : nat) (B : ty) (Gamma : context) : context :=
  match n, Gamma with
  | 0, _       => B :: Gamma
  | S n', h :: t => h :: insert_at n' B t
  | S n', []   => []
  end.

(* ================================================================= *)
(* 2. PROPERTIES OF LIFTING                                          *)
(* ================================================================= *)

(**
 * Lemma: lift_0
 * Statement: lift 0 k t = t
 * Description: Lifting by 0 is the identity operation.
 *)
Lemma lift_0 : forall t k, lift 0 k t = t.
Proof. Admitted.

(**
 * Lemma: lift_add
 * Statement: lift n k (lift m k t) = lift (n + m) k t
 * Description: Composition of lifting is additive.
 *)
Lemma lift_add : forall t n m k, lift n k (lift m k t) = lift (n + m) k t.
Proof. Admitted.

(* ================================================================= *)
(* 3. WEAKENING THEOREMS                                             *)
(* ================================================================= *)

(**
 * Theorem: weakening_general
 * Statement: Gamma |- t : A  =>  (insert_at n B Gamma) |- lift 1 n t : A
 * Description: General weakening lemma for insertion at arbitrary position `n`.
 *)
Theorem weakening_general : forall Gamma t A B n,
  Gamma ⊢ t [:] A ->
  (insert_at n B Gamma) ⊢ (lift 1 n t) [:] A.
Proof. Admitted.

(**
 * Lemma: weakening
 * Statement: Gamma |- t : A  =>  (B :: Gamma) |- lift 1 0 t : A
 * Description: Standard weakening at the head of the context.
 *)
Lemma weakening : forall Gamma t A B,
  Gamma ⊢ t [:] A ->
  (B :: Gamma) ⊢ (lift 1 0 t) [:] A.
Proof. Admitted.

(**
 * Lemma: weakening_from_nil
 * Statement: nil |- t : A  =>  Gamma |- lift |Gamma| 0 t : A
 * Description: Weakening a closed term into an arbitrary context `Gamma`.
 * Requires shifting indices by the length of the new context.
 *)
Lemma weakening_from_nil : forall Gamma t A,
  nil ⊢ t [:] A ->
  Gamma ⊢ (lift (length Gamma) 0 t) [:] A.
Proof. Admitted.--- FILE: skeleton/Logic/STLC_PI/STLC_PI.v ---
Require Import List PeanoNat Arith Lia.
Import ListNotations.
Require Import Coq.Classes.Morphisms.

(* ================================================================= *)
(* TYPES AND TERMS                                *)
(* ================================================================= *)

Inductive ty : Set :=
  | Ty_Atomic : nat -> ty
  | Ty_Unit   : ty
  | Ty_Arrow  : ty -> ty -> ty
  | Ty_Prod   : ty -> ty -> ty.

Inductive tm : Set :=
  | tm_unit : tm
  | tm_var  : nat -> tm
  | tm_lam  : ty -> tm -> tm
  | tm_app  : tm -> tm -> tm
  | tm_pair : tm -> tm -> tm
  | tm_fst  : tm -> tm
  | tm_snd  : tm -> tm.

Definition context := list ty.

(* ================================================================= *)
(* SCOPE DEFINITION                               *)
(* ================================================================= *)

(* Létrehozzuk a saját scope-ot, hogy ne akadjon össze a későbbi nyelvekkel *)
Declare Scope stlc_scope.
Delimit Scope stlc_scope with stlc.

(* ================================================================= *)
(* TYPING SYSTEM (LOGIC LAYER - Prop)                     *)
(* ================================================================= *)
(* Ez a matematikai definíció, ez kell a Kategóriához és Completenesshez *)

Inductive typeability : context -> tm -> ty -> Prop :=
  | T_Unit : forall Gamma,
      typeability Gamma tm_unit Ty_Unit

  | T_Var0 : forall Gamma A,
      typeability (A :: Gamma) (tm_var 0) A

  | T_VarS : forall Gamma A B i,
      typeability Gamma (tm_var i) A ->
      typeability (B :: Gamma) (tm_var (S i)) A

  | T_Lam : forall Gamma t A B,
      typeability (A :: Gamma) t B ->
      typeability Gamma (tm_lam A t) (Ty_Arrow A B)

  | T_App : forall Gamma t1 t2 A B,
      typeability Gamma t1 (Ty_Arrow A B) ->
      typeability Gamma t2 A ->
      typeability Gamma (tm_app t1 t2) B

  | T_Pair : forall Gamma t1 t2 A B,
      typeability Gamma t1 A ->
      typeability Gamma t2 B ->
      typeability Gamma (tm_pair t1 t2) (Ty_Prod A B)

  | T_Fst : forall Gamma t A B,
      typeability Gamma t (Ty_Prod A B) ->
      typeability Gamma (tm_fst t) A

  | T_Snd : forall Gamma t A B,
      typeability Gamma t (Ty_Prod A B) ->
      typeability Gamma (tm_snd t) B.

(* A notációt az stlc_scope-hoz kötjük *)
Notation "Gamma '⊢' t '[:]' A" := (typeability Gamma t A) (at level 70, no associativity) : stlc_scope.
Notation "'⊢' t '[:]' A" := (typeability nil t A) (at level 70, no associativity) : stlc_scope.
Notation "A ⊃ B" := (Ty_Arrow A B) (at level 70, right associativity) : stlc_scope.

(* Alapértelmezetten megnyitjuk a scope-ot ebben a fájlban *)
Open Scope stlc_scope.

(* ================================================================= *)
(* DERIVATION TREES (DATA LAYER - Set)                      *)
(* ================================================================= *)
(* Ez a Soundness-hez (Computation) kell. Ugyanaz a struktúra, de Set-ben. *)

Inductive derivation : context -> tm -> ty -> Set :=
  | D_Unit : forall Gamma,
      derivation Gamma tm_unit Ty_Unit

  | D_Var0 : forall Gamma A,
      derivation (A :: Gamma) (tm_var 0) A

  | D_VarS : forall Gamma A B i,
      derivation Gamma (tm_var i) A ->
      derivation (B :: Gamma) (tm_var (S i)) A

  | D_Lam : forall Gamma t A B,
      derivation (A :: Gamma) t B ->
      derivation Gamma (tm_lam A t) (Ty_Arrow A B)

  | D_App : forall Gamma t1 t2 A B,
      derivation Gamma t1 (Ty_Arrow A B) ->
      derivation Gamma t2 A ->
      derivation Gamma (tm_app t1 t2) B

  | D_Pair : forall Gamma t1 t2 A B,
      derivation Gamma t1 A ->
      derivation Gamma t2 B ->
      derivation Gamma (tm_pair t1 t2) (Ty_Prod A B)

  | D_Fst : forall Gamma t A B,
      derivation Gamma t (Ty_Prod A B) ->
      derivation Gamma (tm_fst t) A

  | D_Snd : forall Gamma t A B,
      derivation Gamma t (Ty_Prod A B) ->
      derivation Gamma (tm_snd t) B.

(* Híd a Derivation (Set) és Typeability (Prop) között *)
Fixpoint forget_derivation {Gamma t A} (d : derivation Gamma t A) : typeability Gamma t A.
Proof. Admitted.

(* ================================================================= *)
(* PROOF IRRELEVANCE LOGIC                          *)
(* ================================================================= *)

Inductive tm_equiv_rel : tm -> tm -> Prop :=
  | PI_rule : forall Gamma A t s,
      (Gamma ⊢ t [:] A) -> (Gamma ⊢ s [:] A) -> tm_equiv_rel t s.

Definition obj_stlc := ty.

Definition hom_stlc (x y : obj_stlc) := { t : tm | ⊢ t [:] (x ⊃ y)}.

Definition hom_equiv_stlc {x y : obj_stlc} (f g : hom_stlc x y) :=
  tm_equiv_rel (proj1_sig f) (proj1_sig g).

Lemma hom_equiv_stlc_refl : forall {x y} (f : hom_stlc x y), hom_equiv_stlc f f.
Proof. Admitted.

Lemma hom_equiv_stlc_sym : forall {x y} (f g : hom_stlc x y), hom_equiv_stlc f g -> hom_equiv_stlc g f.
Proof. Admitted.

Lemma hom_equiv_stlc_trans : forall {x y} (f g h : hom_stlc x y),
  hom_equiv_stlc f g -> hom_equiv_stlc g h -> hom_equiv_stlc f h.
Proof. Admitted.

Lemma generic_axiom_proof : forall {x y} (f g : hom_stlc x y), hom_equiv_stlc f g.
Proof. Admitted.--- FILE: skeleton/Logic/STLC_PI_Sum/STLC_PI_Sum.v ---
Require Import List PeanoNat Arith Lia.
Import ListNotations.
Require Import Coq.Classes.Morphisms.

(* ================================================================= *)
(* TYPES AND TERMS                                                 *)
(* ================================================================= *)

Inductive ty : Set :=
  | Ty_Atomic : nat -> ty
  | Ty_Unit   : ty                 (* Terminal Object (1) *)
  | Ty_Void   : ty                 (* Initial Object (0) *)
  | Ty_Arrow  : ty -> ty -> ty     (* Exponential (=>) *)
  | Ty_Prod   : ty -> ty -> ty     (* Product (⋅) *)
  | Ty_Sum    : ty -> ty -> ty.    (* Coproduct (+) *)

Inductive tm : Set :=
  | tm_unit   : tm
  | tm_var    : nat -> tm
  | tm_lam    : ty -> tm -> tm
  | tm_app    : tm -> tm -> tm
  | tm_pair   : tm -> tm -> tm
  | tm_fst    : tm -> tm
  | tm_snd    : tm -> tm
  (* Extensions for BiCCC *)
  (* We annotate Injections with the type of the *other* side for unique typing *)
  | tm_inl    : ty -> tm -> tm     (* tm_inl B t : A + B (where t:A) *)
  | tm_inr    : ty -> tm -> tm     (* tm_inr A t : A + B (where t:B) *)
  | tm_case   : tm -> tm -> tm -> tm (* case t of (x.t1) | (y.t2) *)
  | tm_absurd : ty -> tm -> tm.    (* explicit type A, term of Void -> A *)

Definition context := list ty.

(* ================================================================= *)
(* SCOPE DEFINITION                                                *)
(* ================================================================= *)

Declare Scope stlc_scope_sum.
Delimit Scope stlc_scope_sum with stlc_sum.

(* ================================================================= *)
(* TYPING SYSTEM (LOGIC LAYER - Prop)                              *)
(* ================================================================= *)

Inductive typeability : context -> tm -> ty -> Prop :=
  | T_Unit : forall Gamma,
      typeability Gamma tm_unit Ty_Unit

  | T_Var0 : forall Gamma A,
      typeability (A :: Gamma) (tm_var 0) A

  | T_VarS : forall Gamma A B i,
      typeability Gamma (tm_var i) A ->
      typeability (B :: Gamma) (tm_var (S i)) A

  | T_Lam : forall Gamma t A B,
      typeability (A :: Gamma) t B ->
      typeability Gamma (tm_lam A t) (Ty_Arrow A B)

  | T_App : forall Gamma t1 t2 A B,
      typeability Gamma t1 (Ty_Arrow A B) ->
      typeability Gamma t2 A ->
      typeability Gamma (tm_app t1 t2) B

  | T_Pair : forall Gamma t1 t2 A B,
      typeability Gamma t1 A ->
      typeability Gamma t2 B ->
      typeability Gamma (tm_pair t1 t2) (Ty_Prod A B)

  | T_Fst : forall Gamma t A B,
      typeability Gamma t (Ty_Prod A B) ->
      typeability Gamma (tm_fst t) A

  | T_Snd : forall Gamma t A B,
      typeability Gamma t (Ty_Prod A B) ->
      typeability Gamma (tm_snd t) B

  (* --- Rules for Sums (Coproducts) --- *)

  | T_Inl : forall Gamma t A B,
      typeability Gamma t A ->
      (* We explicitly state B comes from the annotation *)
      typeability Gamma (tm_inl B t) (Ty_Sum A B)

  | T_Inr : forall Gamma t A B,
      typeability Gamma t B ->
      (* We explicitly state A comes from the annotation *)
      typeability Gamma (tm_inr A t) (Ty_Sum A B)

  | T_Case : forall Gamma t t1 t2 A B C,
      typeability Gamma t (Ty_Sum A B) ->
      typeability (A :: Gamma) t1 C -> (* t1 binds a variable of type A *)
      typeability (B :: Gamma) t2 C -> (* t2 binds a variable of type B *)
      typeability Gamma (tm_case t t1 t2) C

  (* --- Rule for Void (Initial) --- *)

  | T_Absurd : forall Gamma t A,
      typeability Gamma t Ty_Void ->
      typeability Gamma (tm_absurd A t) A.

(* Notation definitions *)
Notation "Gamma '⊢' t '[:]' A" := (typeability Gamma t A) (at level 70, no associativity) : stlc_scope_sum.
Notation "'⊢' t '[:]' A" := (typeability nil t A) (at level 70, no associativity) : stlc_scope_sum.

Notation "A ⊃ B" := (Ty_Arrow A B) (at level 70, right associativity) : stlc_scope_sum.
Notation "A ⊕ B" := (Ty_Sum A B) (at level 50, left associativity) : stlc_scope_sum.
Notation "∅" := (Ty_Void) (at level 0) : stlc_scope_sum.

Open Scope stlc_scope_sum.

(* ================================================================= *)
(* DERIVATION TREES (DATA LAYER - Set)                             *)
(* ================================================================= *)

Inductive derivation : context -> tm -> ty -> Set :=
  | D_Unit : forall Gamma,
      derivation Gamma tm_unit Ty_Unit

  | D_Var0 : forall Gamma A,
      derivation (A :: Gamma) (tm_var 0) A

  | D_VarS : forall Gamma A B i,
      derivation Gamma (tm_var i) A ->
      derivation (B :: Gamma) (tm_var (S i)) A

  | D_Lam : forall Gamma t A B,
      derivation (A :: Gamma) t B ->
      derivation Gamma (tm_lam A t) (Ty_Arrow A B)

  | D_App : forall Gamma t1 t2 A B,
      derivation Gamma t1 (Ty_Arrow A B) ->
      derivation Gamma t2 A ->
      derivation Gamma (tm_app t1 t2) B

  | D_Pair : forall Gamma t1 t2 A B,
      derivation Gamma t1 A ->
      derivation Gamma t2 B ->
      derivation Gamma (tm_pair t1 t2) (Ty_Prod A B)

  | D_Fst : forall Gamma t A B,
      derivation Gamma t (Ty_Prod A B) ->
      derivation Gamma (tm_fst t) A

  | D_Snd : forall Gamma t A B,
      derivation Gamma t (Ty_Prod A B) ->
      derivation Gamma (tm_snd t) B

  (* --- Derivations for Sums --- *)

  | D_Inl : forall Gamma t A B,
      derivation Gamma t A ->
      derivation Gamma (tm_inl B t) (Ty_Sum A B)

  | D_Inr : forall Gamma t A B,
      derivation Gamma t B ->
      derivation Gamma (tm_inr A t) (Ty_Sum A B)

  | D_Case : forall Gamma t t1 t2 A B C,
      derivation Gamma t (Ty_Sum A B) ->
      derivation (A :: Gamma) t1 C ->
      derivation (B :: Gamma) t2 C ->
      derivation Gamma (tm_case t t1 t2) C

  (* --- Derivation for Void --- *)

  | D_Absurd : forall Gamma t A,
      derivation Gamma t Ty_Void ->
      derivation Gamma (tm_absurd A t) A.

(* Bridge between Derivation (Set) and Typeability (Prop) *)
Fixpoint forget_derivation {Gamma t A} (d : derivation Gamma t A) : typeability Gamma t A.
Proof. Admitted.

(* ================================================================= *)
(* PROOF IRRELEVANCE LOGIC                                         *)
(* ================================================================= *)

Inductive tm_equiv_rel : tm -> tm -> Prop :=
  | PI_rule : forall Gamma A t s,
      (Gamma ⊢ t [:] A) -> (Gamma ⊢ s [:] A) -> tm_equiv_rel t s.

Definition obj_stlc := ty.

Definition hom_stlc (x y : obj_stlc) := { t : tm | ⊢ t [:] (x ⊃ y)}.

Definition hom_equiv_stlc {x y : obj_stlc} (f g : hom_stlc x y) :=
  tm_equiv_rel (proj1_sig f) (proj1_sig g).

Lemma hom_equiv_stlc_refl : forall {x y} (f : hom_stlc x y), hom_equiv_stlc f f.
Proof. Admitted.

Lemma hom_equiv_stlc_sym : forall {x y} (f g : hom_stlc x y), hom_equiv_stlc f g -> hom_equiv_stlc g f.
Proof. Admitted.

Lemma hom_equiv_stlc_trans : forall {x y} (f g h : hom_stlc x y),
  hom_equiv_stlc f g -> hom_equiv_stlc g h -> hom_equiv_stlc f h.
Proof. Admitted.

Lemma generic_axiom_proof : forall {x y} (f g : hom_stlc x y), hom_equiv_stlc f g.
Proof. Admitted.--- FILE: skeleton/Logic/STLC_PI_Sum/STLC_PI_Sum_Lemmas.v ---
Require Import List Arith PeanoNat Lia.
Import ListNotations.
Require Import STLC_PI_Sum.

Open Scope stlc_scope_sum.

(* ================================================================= *)
(* 1. SYNTAX OPERATIONS                                            *)
(* ================================================================= *)

(**
 * Operation: lift
 * Description: Shifts De Bruijn indices to handle variable binding.
 * Extended for Sums and Void.
 *)
Fixpoint lift (n : nat) (k : nat) (t : tm) : tm :=
  match t with
  | tm_unit => tm_unit
  | tm_var i =>
      if k <=? i then tm_var (n + i) else tm_var i
  | tm_lam A t' =>
      tm_lam A (lift n (S k) t')
  | tm_app t1 t2 =>
      tm_app (lift n k t1) (lift n k t2)
  | tm_pair t1 t2 =>
      tm_pair (lift n k t1) (lift n k t2)
  | tm_fst t' =>
      tm_fst (lift n k t')
  | tm_snd t' =>
      tm_snd (lift n k t')

  (* --- New Cases --- *)
  | tm_inl B t' =>
      tm_inl B (lift n k t')
  | tm_inr A t' =>
      tm_inr A (lift n k t')
  | tm_case t t1 t2 =>
      (* t has no new binders.
         t1 binds a variable (the Left case), so we increment k.
         t2 binds a variable (the Right case), so we increment k. *)
      tm_case (lift n k t) (lift n (S k) t1) (lift n (S k) t2)
  | tm_absurd A t' =>
      tm_absurd A (lift n k t')
  end.

(**
 * Operation: insert_at
 * Description: Inserts a type `B` into context `Gamma` at position `n`.
 *)
Fixpoint insert_at (n : nat) (B : ty) (Gamma : context) : context :=
  match n, Gamma with
  | 0, _       => B :: Gamma
  | S n', h :: t => h :: insert_at n' B t
  | S n', []   => []
  end.

(* ================================================================= *)
(* 2. PROPERTIES OF LIFTING                                        *)
(* ================================================================= *)

Lemma lift_0 : forall t k, lift 0 k t = t.
Proof. Admitted.

Lemma lift_add : forall t n m k, lift n k (lift m k t) = lift (n + m) k t.
Proof. Admitted.

(* ================================================================= *)
(* 3. WEAKENING THEOREMS                                           *)
(* ================================================================= *)

Theorem weakening_general : forall Gamma t A B n,
  Gamma ⊢ t [:] A ->
  (insert_at n B Gamma) ⊢ (lift 1 n t) [:] A.
Proof. Admitted.

Lemma weakening : forall Gamma t A B,
  Gamma ⊢ t [:] A ->
  (B :: Gamma) ⊢ (lift 1 0 t) [:] A.
Proof. Admitted.

Lemma weakening_from_nil : forall Gamma t A,
  nil ⊢ t [:] A ->
  Gamma ⊢ (lift (length Gamma) 0 t) [:] A.
Proof. Admitted.

Lemma weakening_append : forall Gamma Delta t A,
  Gamma ⊢ t [:] A -> (Gamma ++ Delta) ⊢ t [:] A.
Proof. Admitted.--- FILE: skeleton/Semantics/Epsilon/Epsilon_Adjoint_Fibration.v ---

Class AdjointEpsilonFibration
  {Sign : Type} {SM : SkolemMapping Sign}
  {C : SetoidCategory} {CC : CartesianCategory C} {TSC : TwoSortCategory C}
  {BEM : BaseEpsilonModel} {GF : GoodFibration C}
  {SGF : StrictGoodFibration C GF}
  {EHM : EpsilonHyperModel SGF} := {

    eps_is_left_adj_obj : forall (I A : b_ty) (Phi : @h_ty Sign),
    @left_adj_obj _ _ _ (pullback_has_left_adjoint (interp_tm (BT_Fst (Sign:=Sign) (A:=I) (B:=A))))
      (interp_h_ty (b_Prod I A) Phi)
    ≅O
    interp_h_ty I (form_pullback (b_pair b_id (b_const A (skolem_index A Phi))) Phi) ;

    eps_functorial : forall (I A : b_ty) (Phi Psi : @h_ty Sign),
    interp_h_ty (b_Prod I A) Phi ≅O interp_h_ty (b_Prod I A) Psi ->
    interp_h_ty I (form_pullback (b_pair b_id (b_const A (skolem_index A Phi))) Phi)
    ≅O
    interp_h_ty I (form_pullback (b_pair b_id (b_const A (skolem_index A Psi))) Psi)
}.--- FILE: skeleton/Semantics/Epsilon/Epsilon_Fibration.v ---

(* ================================================================= *)
(* 1. BÁZIS SZEMANTIKA (Epszilon Konstansok)                         *)
(* ================================================================= *)

(** A bázis típusok kategóriaelméleti interpretációja.
    Ez felelteti meg a szintaktikus b_ty elemeit a kategória objektumainak. *)
Fixpoint interp_ty {C : SetoidCategory} {CC : CartesianCategory C} {TSC : TwoSortCategory C}
  (A : b_ty) : @obj C :=
  match A with
  | b_Unit => terminal
  | b_S => @S C CC TSC   (* <--- ITT A JAVÍTÁS: Mindent expliciten átadunk! *)
  | b_Prod A B => prod (interp_ty A) (interp_ty B)
  end.

(** A bázis modellnek csak annyi a dolga, hogy minden Sign-beli szimbólumhoz
    (amit a skolem_index generál) hozzárendeljen egy bázismorfizmust. *)
Class BaseEpsilonModel {Sign : Type} {C : SetoidCategory} {CC : CartesianCategory C} {TSC : TwoSortCategory C} := {
  i_const : forall (I A : b_ty) (s : Sign), @hom C (interp_ty I) (interp_ty A)
}.

(** Az interp_tm kiterjesztése az új BT_Const szabállyal. *)
Fixpoint interp_tm {Sign : Type} {C : SetoidCategory} {CC : CartesianCategory C}
  {TSC : TwoSortCategory C} {BEM : BaseEpsilonModel}
  {I A : b_ty} {f : @b_tm Sign} (d : @base_typing Sign I f A)
  : @hom C (interp_ty I) (interp_ty A) :=
  match d in (base_typing I' f' A') return (@hom C (interp_ty I') (interp_ty A')) with
  | @BT_Id _ A' => @id C (interp_ty A')

  (* JAVÍTVA: Itt a (interp_tm dg) és (interp_tm df) sorrendjét megcseréltük! *)
  | @BT_Comp _ A' B' C' g' f' dg df => @compose C (interp_ty A') (interp_ty B') (interp_ty C') (interp_tm dg) (interp_tm df)

  | @BT_Unit _ A' => @terminal_map C CC (interp_ty A')
  | @BT_Pair _ A' B' C' f' g' df dg => @prod_map C CC (interp_ty A') (interp_ty B') (interp_ty C') (interp_tm df) (interp_tm dg)
  | @BT_Fst _ A' B' => @prod_fst C CC (interp_ty A') (interp_ty B')
  | @BT_Snd _ A' B' => @prod_snd C CC (interp_ty A') (interp_ty B')
  | @BT_Const _ I' A' s => i_const I' A' s
  end.

(* ================================================================= *)
(* 2. HYPER SZEMANTIKA (Atomok és Kvantorok)                         *)
(* ================================================================= *)

(** Az EpsilonHyperModel az atomok interpretációját rögzíti, ahogy a Simple logikában is. *)
Class EpsilonHyperModel
  {Sign : Type}
  {C : SetoidCategory} {CC : CartesianCategory C} {TSC : TwoSortCategory C}
  {BEM : BaseEpsilonModel}  (* <--- ITT A JAVÍTÁS: Szükségünk van a bázismodellre az interp_tm-hez! *)
  {GF : GoodFibration C} (SGF : StrictGoodFibration C GF) := {

  i_atom : forall (p : nat) (t : @b_tm Sign) (I : b_ty), @obj (Fiber (interp_ty I)) ;

  i_atom_pb : forall (p : nat) (t : @b_tm Sign) (I A : b_ty) (f : @b_tm Sign) (df : @base_typing Sign I f A),
      @fobj _ _ (Pullback (interp_tm df)) (i_atom p t A) ≅O i_atom p (b_comp t f) I;

  i_atom_cong_global : forall (p : nat) (I_target I_orig A : b_ty) (t1 t2 : b_tm)
                              (d1 : base_typing I_orig t1 A) (d2 : base_typing I_orig t2 A),
                       base_equiv d1 d2 -> i_atom p t1 I_target ≅O i_atom p t2 I_target
                       (*
  i_atom_cong : forall (p : nat) (I A : b_ty) (t1 t2 : b_tm)
                       (d1 : base_typing I t1 A) (d2 : base_typing I t2 A),
                base_equiv d1 d2 -> i_atom p t1 I ≅O i_atom p t2 I *)  }.

(** A formulák interpretációja, amely már tartalmazza a Ty_All és Ty_Ex kvantorokat is! *)
Fixpoint interp_h_ty
  {Sign : Type} {C : SetoidCategory} {CC : CartesianCategory C} {TSC : TwoSortCategory C}
  {BEM : BaseEpsilonModel} {GF : GoodFibration C} {SGF : StrictGoodFibration C GF}
  {EHM : EpsilonHyperModel SGF}
  (I : b_ty) (Phi : @h_ty Sign) : @obj (Fiber (interp_ty I)) :=
  match Phi with
  | Ty_Unit => terminal
  | Ty_Void => initial
  | Ty_Prod A B => prod (interp_h_ty I A) (interp_h_ty I B)
  | Ty_Sum A B => coprod (interp_h_ty I A) (interp_h_ty I B)
  | Ty_Arrow A B => exp (interp_h_ty I B) (interp_h_ty I A)
  | Ty_Atom p t => i_atom p t I
  | Ty_All A Body =>
      @right_adj_obj _ _ _ pullback_has_right_adjoint
                           (interp_h_ty (b_Prod I A) Body)
  | Ty_Ex A Body =>
      @left_adj_obj _ _ _ pullback_has_left_adjoint
                          (interp_h_ty (b_Prod I A) Body)
                          end.

Fixpoint interp_h_ctx
  {Sign : Type} {C : SetoidCategory} {CC : CartesianCategory C} {TSC : TwoSortCategory C}
  {BEM : BaseEpsilonModel} {GF : GoodFibration C} {SGF : StrictGoodFibration C GF}
  {EHM : EpsilonHyperModel SGF}
  (I : b_ty) (G : @h_context Sign) : @obj (Fiber (interp_ty I)) :=
  match G with
  | nil => terminal
  | Phi :: G' => prod (interp_h_ctx I G') (interp_h_ty I Phi)
  end.

(* ================================================================= *)
(* 3. INTENZIONÁLIS EPSZILON LOGIKA (Pszeudo-Adjungált)              *)
(* ================================================================= *)

(** Itt jön a mágia! Nem definiálunk funktort. Csak explicit szintaktikai formulákra
    (Phi : @h_ty Sign) követeljük meg az Intro (Unit) és Elim (Frobenius) dedukciós szabályokat,
    amelyeket a skolem_index orákulum köt a bázishoz. *)

Class EpsilonGoodFibration
  {Sign : Type} {SM : SkolemMapping Sign}
  {C : SetoidCategory} {CC : CartesianCategory C} {TSC : TwoSortCategory C}
  {BEM : BaseEpsilonModel} {GF : GoodFibration C} {SGF : StrictGoodFibration C GF}
  {EHM : EpsilonHyperModel SGF} := {

  (** 1. EPSILON INTRO (Unit) *)
  eps_unit : forall (I A : b_ty) (Phi : @h_ty Sign),
      hom (interp_h_ty (b_Prod I A) Phi)
          (interp_h_ty (b_Prod I A)
             (form_pullback b_fst
                (form_pullback (b_pair b_id (b_const A (skolem_index A Phi))) Phi))) ;

  (** 2. EPSILON ELIM (Frobenius Reciprocitás) *)
  eps_elim_frobenius : forall (I A : b_ty) (G : @h_context Sign) (Phi Chi : @h_ty Sign),
      hom (prod (interp_h_ctx (b_Prod I A) (ctx_pullback b_fst G)) (interp_h_ty (b_Prod I A) Phi))
          (interp_h_ty (b_Prod I A) (form_pullback b_fst Chi)) ->
      hom (prod (interp_h_ctx I G)
                (interp_h_ty I (form_pullback (b_pair b_id (b_const A (skolem_index A Phi))) Phi)))
          (interp_h_ty I Chi) ;

  eps_beck_chevalley_iso : forall (I J A : b_ty) (g : b_tm) (dg : base_typing J g I) (Phi : h_ty),
    interp_h_ty J (form_pullback g (form_pullback (b_pair b_id (b_const A (skolem_index A Phi))) Phi)) ≅O
    interp_h_ty J (form_pullback (b_pair (b_comp g b_fst) b_snd) Phi .[ ε A, form_pullback (b_pair (b_comp g b_fst) b_snd) Phi ])
}.--- FILE: skeleton/Semantics/Epsilon/Epsilon_Soundness.v ---

(* ================================================================= *)
(* 1. STRUKTURÁLIS KOMPATIBILITÁSI LEMMÁK (AXIÓMÁK)                  *)
(* ================================================================= *)

Lemma typing_pullback_ext :
  forall {Sign : Type} {I J b : b_ty} {g : @b_tm Sign} (dg : base_typing I g J),
  base_typing (b_Prod I b) (b_pullback_ext g) (b_Prod J b).
Proof. Admitted.

Lemma form_pullback_general_iso :
  forall {Sign : Type} {SM : SkolemMapping Sign}
         {C : SetoidCategory} {CC : CartesianCategory C} {TSC : TwoSortCategory C}
         {BEM : BaseEpsilonModel} {GF : GoodFibration C} {SGF : StrictGoodFibration C GF}
         {EHM : EpsilonHyperModel SGF} {EGF : EpsilonGoodFibration}
         (Phi : @h_ty Sign) (I J : b_ty) (g : @b_tm Sign) (dg : base_typing I g J),
  interp_h_ty I (form_pullback g Phi) ≅O
  @fobj _ _ (Pullback (interp_tm dg)) (interp_h_ty J Phi).
Proof. Admitted.

(** * Syntactic Substitution = Semantic Pullback *)
Lemma form_pullback_iso :
  forall {Sign : Type} {SM : SkolemMapping Sign}
         {C : SetoidCategory} {CC : CartesianCategory C} {TSC : TwoSortCategory C}
         {BEM : BaseEpsilonModel} {GF : GoodFibration C} {SGF : StrictGoodFibration C GF}
         {EHM : EpsilonHyperModel SGF} {EGF : EpsilonGoodFibration}
         (I A : b_ty) (f : @b_tm Sign) (df : base_typing I f A) (Phi : @h_ty Sign),
  interp_h_ty I (form_pullback (b_pair b_id f) Phi) ≅O
  @fobj _ _ (Pullback (interp_tm (BT_Pair BT_Id df))) (interp_h_ty (b_Prod I A) Phi).
Proof. Admitted.

Lemma form_pullback_fst_iso :
  forall {Sign : Type} {SM : SkolemMapping Sign}
         {C : SetoidCategory} {CC : CartesianCategory C} {TSC : TwoSortCategory C}
         {BEM : BaseEpsilonModel} {GF : GoodFibration C} {SGF : StrictGoodFibration C GF}
         {EHM : EpsilonHyperModel SGF} {EGF : EpsilonGoodFibration}
         (I A : b_ty) (Phi : @h_ty Sign),
  interp_h_ty (b_Prod I A) (form_pullback b_fst Phi) ≅O
  @fobj _ _ (Pullback (interp_tm (BT_Fst (A:=I) (B:=A)))) (interp_h_ty I Phi).
Proof. Admitted.

Lemma ctx_pullback_iso :
  forall {Sign : Type} {SM : SkolemMapping Sign}
         {C : SetoidCategory} {CC : CartesianCategory C} {TSC : TwoSortCategory C}
         {BEM : BaseEpsilonModel} {GF : GoodFibration C} {SGF : StrictGoodFibration C GF}
         {EHM : EpsilonHyperModel SGF} {EGF : EpsilonGoodFibration}
         (I A : b_ty) (G : @h_context Sign),
  interp_h_ctx (b_Prod I A) (ctx_pullback b_fst G) ≅O
  @fobj _ _ (Pullback (interp_tm (BT_Fst (A:=I) (B:=A)))) (interp_h_ctx I G).
Proof. Admitted.

(* ================================================================= *)
(* 1.5 FORMULA EKVIVALENCIA = KATEGÓRIAELMÉLETI IZOMORFIZMUS         *)
(* ================================================================= *)

(* 1. Axióma: A szintaktikai identitás visszahúzás izomorf az eredetivel *)

Lemma interp_pb_cong : forall {Sign : Type} {SM : SkolemMapping Sign}
  {C : SetoidCategory} {CC : CartesianCategory C} {BCCC : CartesianClosedCategory C}
  {BiCCC : BiCartesianClosedCategory BCCC} {TSC : TwoSortCategory C}
  {BEM : BaseEpsilonModel} {GF : GoodFibration C} {SGF : StrictGoodFibration C GF}
  {EHM : EpsilonHyperModel SGF} (I J : b_ty) (f g : @b_tm Sign) (Phi : @h_ty Sign)
  (df : base_typing I f J) (dg : base_typing I g J),
  base_equiv df dg ->
  WellFormed J Phi ->
  (interp_h_ty I (form_pullback f Phi)) ≅O (interp_h_ty I (form_pullback g Phi)).
Proof. Admitted.

Lemma interp_pb_id : forall {Sign : Type} {SM : SkolemMapping Sign}
  {C : SetoidCategory} {CC : CartesianCategory C} {BCCC : CartesianClosedCategory C}
  {BiCCC : BiCartesianClosedCategory BCCC} {TSC : TwoSortCategory C}
  {BEM : BaseEpsilonModel} {GF : GoodFibration C} {SGF : StrictGoodFibration C GF}
  {EHM : EpsilonHyperModel SGF} (I : b_ty) (Phi : @h_ty Sign),
  WellFormed I Phi ->
  (interp_h_ty I (form_pullback b_id Phi)) ≅O (interp_h_ty I Phi).
Proof. Admitted.

Lemma interp_pb_comp : forall {Sign : Type} {SM : SkolemMapping Sign}
  {C : SetoidCategory} {CC : CartesianCategory C} {BCCC : CartesianClosedCategory C}
  {BiCCC : BiCartesianClosedCategory BCCC} {TSC : TwoSortCategory C}
  {BEM : BaseEpsilonModel} {GF : GoodFibration C} {SGF : StrictGoodFibration C GF}
  {EHM : EpsilonHyperModel SGF} (I J K: b_ty) (f g : @b_tm Sign) (Phi : @h_ty Sign)
  (df : base_typing J f K) (dg : base_typing I g J),
  WellFormed K Phi ->
  (interp_h_ty I (form_pullback (b_comp f g) Phi)) ≅O (interp_h_ty I (form_pullback g (form_pullback f Phi))).
Proof. Admitted.

Lemma form_equiv_iso :
  forall {Sign : Type} {SM : SkolemMapping Sign}
         {C : SetoidCategory} {CC : CartesianCategory C} {BCCC : CartesianClosedCategory C} {BiCCC : BiCartesianClosedCategory BCCC}
         {TSC : TwoSortCategory C} {BEM : BaseEpsilonModel} {GF : GoodFibration C} {SGF : StrictGoodFibration C GF}
         {EHM : EpsilonHyperModel SGF} {EGF : EpsilonGoodFibration}
         (I : b_ty) (Phi Psi : @h_ty Sign),
  form_equiv I Phi Psi -> interp_h_ty I Phi ≅O interp_h_ty I Psi.
Proof. Admitted.

(* ================================================================= *)
(* 2. INHABITED SOUNDNESS TÉTEL                                      *)
(* ================================================================= *)

Theorem hyper_soundness_inhabited
  {Sign : Type} {SM : SkolemMapping Sign}
  {C : SetoidCategory} {CC : CartesianCategory C} {BCCC : CartesianClosedCategory C} {BiCCC : BiCartesianClosedCategory BCCC}
  {TSC : TwoSortCategory C} {BEM : BaseEpsilonModel} {GF : GoodFibration C} {SGF : StrictGoodFibration C GF}
  {EHM : EpsilonHyperModel SGF} {EGF : EpsilonGoodFibration}
  {I : b_ty} {G : @h_context Sign} {p : @h_tm Sign} {Phi : @h_ty Sign}
  (D : h_derivation I G p Phi) : hom (interp_h_ctx I G) (interp_h_ty I Phi).
Proof. Admitted.--- FILE: skeleton/Semantics/Simple/Example_All_intro.v ---
Require Import Coq.Classes.Morphisms.
Require Import List Arith PeanoNat Lia.
Import ListNotations.

(* Kategóriaelméleti alapok *)
Require Import HyperLogic.Cat.Core.
Require Import HyperLogic.Cat.CC.
Require Import HyperLogic.Cat.CCC.
Require Import HyperLogic.Cat.BCCC.
Require Import HyperLogic.Cat.Functor.
Require Import HyperLogic.Cat.Adjoint.
Require Import HyperLogic.Cat.Fibration.

(* Szintaxis és Bázis Szemantika *)
Require Import HyperLogic.Logic.Base.Simple.Base_Syntax.
Require Import HyperLogic.Semantics.Simple.Base_Simple_Soundness.
Require Import HyperLogic.Logic.STLC_PI_Sum.STLC_PI_Sum.
Require Import HyperLogic.Logic.Hyper.Simple.Hyper_Syntax.
Require Import HyperLogic.Logic.Hyper.Simple.Hyper_Lemmas.
Require Import HyperLogic.Semantics.Simple.Simple_Soundness.

(* =========================================================================
   1. TÁBLÁZAT: SZINTAXIS VS. SZEMANTIKA (HT_All_Intro)
   =========================================================================
   Melyik HyperLogic szintaktikai elem minek felel meg a Fibrációban?

   | Szintaxis (HyperLogic)     | Szemantika (Hiperdoktrína)                   | Magyarázat / Jelentés                                 |
   |----------------------------|----------------------------------------------|-------------------------------------------------------|
   | I, A : b_ty                | I, A ∈ Obj(B)                                | Bázis típusok (Az I a meglévő kontextus, A az új)     |
   | G : h_context              | Γ ∈ Obj(Fiber(I))                            | A feltevéseink (logikai kontextus) az I bázison       |
   | Phi : h_ty                 | Φ ∈ Obj(Fiber(I × A))                        | A bizonyítandó formula (függ I-től és A-tól is)       |
   | b_fst                      | π₁ : I × A → I                               | Bázis projekció (A régi változók megtartása)          |
   | ctx_pullback b_fst G       | π₁^* Γ                                       | A logikai feltevések "felhúzása" az új, tágabb térbe  |
   | Ty_All A Phi               | Π_{π₁} Φ                                     | Univerzális kvantor (A π₁^* jobb adjungáltja)         |
   | HT_All_Intro (maga a tétel)| Hom(π₁^* Γ, Φ) ≅ Hom(Γ, Π_{π₁} Φ)            | Az Adjungálás definíciója (balról jobbra olvasva)     |
   ========================================================================= *)

(* =========================================================================
   2. KOMMUTATÍV DIAGRAM: A KITERJESZTÉS ÉS KVANTIFIKÁLÁS GEOMETRIÁJA
   =========================================================================

   BÁZIS KATEGÓRIA (B) - Itt élnek a változók tartományai
   ------------------------------------------------------
               I × A     <-- Az új "bővített" kontextus (a régi I + az új A változó)
                 |
                 | π₁    <-- A projekció, ami eldobja az A-t és visszatér I-be
                 v
                 I       <-- Az eredeti változó-kontextusunk

   SZÁLAK KATEGÓRIÁI (Fibers) - Itt élnek a formulák és a bizonyítások
   -------------------------------------------------------------------
   Fiber(I × A) :     π₁^* Γ -------- p -------> Φ    <-- PREMISSZA: Van egy 'p' bizonyításunk a tágabb térben.
                        ^                        |
                        |                        |
         Visszahúzás    | π₁^* | Π_{π₁} (Jobb adjungált / Kvantor)
         (Weakening)    |                        |
                        |                        v
   Fiber(I)     :       Γ ------ all_intro ----> Π_{π₁} Φ  <-- KONKLÚZIÓ: Ebből kapunk egy bizonyítást a szűkebb térben.

   Magyarázat: A π₁^* és a Π_{π₁} adjungált párt alkotnak (π₁^* ⊣ Π_{π₁}).
   Ez a matematikai törvény garantálja, hogy a fenti két (p és all_intro) nyíl "kicserélhető" egymással!
   ========================================================================= *)

Section Quantifier_Introduction_Explanation.

  Context {C : SetoidCategory} {CC : CartesianCategory C}
          {GF : GoodFibration C}.

  (* Bázis típusok: I (ahol most vagyunk) és A (a bevezetendő új változó típusa) *)
  Variable I A : @obj C.

  (* ----------------------------------------------------------------- *)
  (* 3. JAVÍTOTT ÉS BIZTONSÁGOS RÖVIDÍTÉSEK (NOTATIONS)                *)
  (* ----------------------------------------------------------------- *)

  (* 1. A π₁ projekció: I x A -> I *)
  Notation "'π₁'" := (@prod_fst C CC I A) (at level 0).

  (* 2. Visszahúzott formula (Weakening). Kifejezetten a formulára (X) alkalmazzuk! *)
  Notation "g '^*' X" := (@fobj _ _ (Pullback g) X) (at level 20, right associativity).

  (* 3. Univerzális Kvantor (Jobb adjungált). Szintén expliciten a formulára (X) alkalmazva! *)
  Notation "'Π_(' g ')' X" := (@right_adj_obj _ _ _ (pullback_has_right_adjoint g) X) (at level 20, right associativity).

  (* ----------------------------------------------------------------- *)
  (* 4. A HT_All_Intro SZEMANTIKAI BIZONYÍTÁSA                         *)
  (* ----------------------------------------------------------------- *)

  (* G a logikai kontextusunk (feltevéseink) az eredeti I bázison *)
  Variable G : @obj (Fiber I).

  (* Phi a formula, amit be akarunk bizonyítani, de ez már függhet az I-től és az A-tól is!
     Ezért ez a Fiber(I x A) szálban él. *)
  Variable Phi : @obj (Fiber (prod I A)).

  (* TÉTEL (HT_All_Intro):
     Ha a felhúzott kontextusból (Weakening) tudjuk bizonyítani a Phi-t az I x A felett,
     akkor az eredeti kontextusból tudjuk bizonyítani a ∀A. Φ-t az I felett.
  *)
  Lemma semantic_All_Intro :
    (* Premissza: A felhúzott kontextus implikálja a Formulát az I x A szálban *)
    hom (π₁ ^* G) Phi ->

    (* Konklúzió: Az eredeti kontextus implikálja a kvantált formulát az I szálban *)
    hom G (Π_(π₁) Phi).
  Proof. Admitted.

End Quantifier_Introduction_Explanation.

Section Forall_And_Distribution.

  (* ========================================================================= *)
  (* 1. RÉSZ: SZINTAKTIKAI BIZONYÍTÁSTERMEK (Point-Free Bázissal)              *)
  (* ========================================================================= *)

  Variable I A : b_ty.
  Variable Phi Psi : h_ty.

  (* Mivel I x A bázison vagyunk az Intro után, a kvantált "x" változó
     nem más, mint a második projekció, ami kiszedi az A-t! *)
  Let var_A : b_tm := b_snd.

  (* ODA IRÁNY: ∀A. (Phi ∧ Psi) ⊢ (∀A. Phi) ∧ (∀A. Psi) *)
  (* Tegyük fel, hogy a 0-ás logikai változónk típusa: ∀A. (Phi ∧ Psi) *)

  Definition term_forall_and_forward : h_tm :=
    tm_pair
      (* Első komponens: ∀A. Phi *)
      (tm_all_intro
         (tm_fst (tm_all_elim (tm_var 0) var_A)))

      (* Második komponens: ∀A. Psi *)
      (tm_all_intro
         (tm_snd (tm_all_elim (tm_var 0) var_A))).

  (* VISSZA IRÁNY: (∀A. Phi) ∧ (∀A. Psi) ⊢ ∀A. (Phi ∧ Psi) *)
  (* Tegyük fel, hogy a 0-ás logikai változónk típusa: (∀A. Phi) ∧ (∀A. Psi) *)

  Definition term_forall_and_backward : h_tm :=
    tm_all_intro
      (tm_pair
         (* Kibontjuk a bal oldali ∀-t a var_A bázis termmel *)
         (tm_all_elim (tm_fst (tm_var 0)) var_A)

         (* Kibontjuk a jobb oldali ∀-t a var_A bázis termmel *)
         (tm_all_elim (tm_snd (tm_var 0)) var_A)).

  (* Megjegyzés: A tényleges 'h_typing' bizonyításokhoz a form_pullback
     egyenlőségeit (L_Cast) kellene használni, de a termek szerkezete tökéletesen
     tükrözi a kategóriaelméleti műveleteket. *)

  (* ========================================================================= *)
  (* 2. RÉSZ: SZEMANTIKAI BIZONYÍTÁS (Adjoints and Limits)                     *)
  (* ========================================================================= *)

  Context {C : SetoidCategory} {CC : CartesianCategory C}
          {GF : GoodFibration C}.

  Variable I_sem A_sem : @obj C.

  Notation "'π₁'" := (@prod_fst C CC I_sem A_sem) (at level 0).
  Notation "g '^*' X" := (@fobj _ _ (Pullback g) X) (at level 20, right associativity).
  Notation "'Π_(' g ')' X" := (@right_adj_obj _ _ _ (pullback_has_right_adjoint g) X) (at level 20, right associativity).

  Variable Phi_sem Psi_sem : @obj (Fiber (prod I_sem A_sem)).

  (* ODA IRÁNY Szemantikája: A right_adj_mor (∀_intro) és a prod_map (tm_pair) legózása *)
  Lemma semantic_forall_and_forward :
    hom (Π_(π₁) (@prod _ (Fiber_is_Cartesian (prod I_sem A_sem)) Phi_sem Psi_sem))
        (@prod _ (Fiber_is_Cartesian I_sem) (Π_(π₁) Phi_sem) (Π_(π₁) Psi_sem)).
  Proof. Admitted.

  (* VISSZA IRÁNY Szemantikája: Szintén right_adj_mor, de itt használjuk a pb_prod_iso axiómát! *)
  Lemma semantic_forall_and_backward :
    hom (@prod _ (Fiber_is_Cartesian I_sem) (Π_(π₁) Phi_sem) (Π_(π₁) Psi_sem))
        (Π_(π₁) (@prod _ (Fiber_is_Cartesian (prod I_sem A_sem)) Phi_sem Psi_sem)).
  Proof. Admitted.

End Forall_And_Distribution.--- FILE: skeleton/Semantics/Simple/Example_All_elim.v ---
Require Import Coq.Classes.Morphisms.
Require Import List Arith PeanoNat Lia.
Import ListNotations.

(* Kategóriaelméleti alapok *)
Require Import HyperLogic.Cat.Core.
Require Import HyperLogic.Cat.CC.
Require Import HyperLogic.Cat.CCC.
Require Import HyperLogic.Cat.BCCC.
Require Import HyperLogic.Cat.Functor.
Require Import HyperLogic.Cat.Adjoint.
Require Import HyperLogic.Cat.Fibration.

(* Szintaxis és Bázis Szemantika *)
Require Import HyperLogic.Logic.Base.Simple.Base_Syntax.
Require Import HyperLogic.Semantics.Simple.Base_Simple_Soundness.
Require Import HyperLogic.Logic.STLC_PI_Sum.STLC_PI_Sum.
Require Import HyperLogic.Logic.Hyper.Simple.Hyper_Syntax.
Require Import HyperLogic.Logic.Hyper.Simple.Hyper_Lemmas.
Require Import HyperLogic.Semantics.Simple.Simple_Soundness.

(* =========================================================================
   1. TÁBLÁZAT: SZINTAXIS VS. SZEMANTIKA (Szótár)
   =========================================================================
   Melyik HyperLogic szintaktikai elem minek felel meg a Fibrációban?

   | Szintaxis (HyperLogic)     | Szemantika (Hiperdoktrína)                   | Magyarázat / Jelentés                                 |
   |----------------------------|----------------------------------------------|-------------------------------------------------------|
   | I, A : b_ty                | I, A ∈ Obj(B)                                | Bázis típusok (Individuumok tartományai)              |
   | Gamma, Phi : h_ty          | Γ, Φ ∈ Obj(Fiber(I))                         | Formulák / Logikai kontextusok az adott szálban       |
   | b_fst                      | π₁ : I × A → I                               | Bázis projekció (Weakening/gyengítés morfizmusa)      |
   | ctx_pullback b_fst G       | π₁^* Γ                                       | A kontextus felhúzása (Weakening funktor)             |
   | Ty_All A Phi               | Π_{π₁} Φ                                     | Univerzális kvantor (A π₁^* jobb adjungáltja)         |
   | f : I -> A (base_typing)   | f : I → A                                    | Bázis morfizmus (Egy konkrét behelyettesítendő term)  |
   | b_pair b_id f              | ⟨id_I, f⟩ : I → I × A                        | A behelyettesítés gráf-morfizmusa a bázisban          |
   | form_pullback <id, f> Phi  | ⟨id_I, f⟩^* Φ                                | Szintaktikus behelyettesítés: Φ[f/A]                  |
   | HT_All_Intro               | Hom(π₁^* Γ, Φ) ≅ Hom(Γ, Π_{π₁} Φ)            | Adjungálás (Bizonyítás felhúzott kontextusból)        |
   | HT_All_Elim                | ε : π₁^* Π_{π₁} Φ → Φ  (+ Pullback)          | Kvantor elimináció (A Counit / Kiértékelés)           |
   ========================================================================= *)

(* =========================================================================
   2. KOMMUTATÍV DIAGRAM: A BEHELYETTESÍTÉS GEOMETRIÁJA
   =========================================================================

   BÁZIS KATEGÓRIA (B) - Itt élnek a változók és a termek
   ------------------------------------------------------
               I × A
              /  ^
         π₁  /    \  ⟨id_I, f⟩  <-- Ez a "f" term behelyettesítése!
            /      \
           v        \
           I <------- I
               id_I

   (Megjegyzés: π₁ ∘ ⟨id_I, f⟩ = id_I, azaz a behelyettesítés után a bázis nem változik)

   SZÁLAK KATEGÓRIÁI (Fibers) - Itt élnek a formulák és a bizonyítások
   -------------------------------------------------------------------
   Fiber(I × A) :     π₁^* Γ                Φ      <-- Az "∀ Intro" premisszája
                        |                   ^
                        | π₁^* | π₁^*
                        v                   |
   Fiber(I)     :       Γ -----------> Π_{π₁} Φ    <-- Maga a kvantált formula (∀A. Φ)
                                  \
                                   \ ⟨id_I, f⟩^* (Visszahúzás = Behelyettesítés)
                                    v
                               ⟨id_I, f⟩^* Φ       <-- A konklúzió: Φ[f/A]
   ========================================================================= *)

Section Quantifier_Elimination_Explanation.

  Context {C : SetoidCategory} {CC : CartesianCategory C}
          {GF : GoodFibration C}.

  (* Bázis típusok: I (ahol most vagyunk) és A (a kvantált változó típusa) *)
  Variable I A : @obj C.

  (* ----------------------------------------------------------------- *)
  (* 3. PLASZTIKUS RÖVIDÍTÉSEK (NOTATIONS)                             *)
  (* ----------------------------------------------------------------- *)

  (* 1. A π₁ projekció: I x A -> I *)
  Notation "'π₁'" := (@prod_fst C CC I A) (at level 0).

 (* A HELYES VISSZAHÚZÁS: Egyértelműen meghívja az 'fobj'-t a Pullback funktoron *)
  Notation "g '^*' X" := (@fobj _ _ (Pullback g) X) (at level 20, right associativity).

  (* Kvantált formula *)
  Notation "'Π_(' g ')' X" := (@right_adj_obj _ _ _ (pullback_has_right_adjoint g) X) (at level 20, right associativity).

  Notation "'⟨' g ',' h '⟩'" := (@prod_map C CC _ _ _ g h) (at level 0).

  (* ================================================================= *)
  (* BIZONYÍTÁS                                                        *)
  (* ================================================================= *)

  Variable G : @obj (Fiber I).
  Variable Phi : @obj (Fiber (prod I A)).
  Variable f : hom I A.

  (*
  Ahhoz, hogy a Φ formulában az A helyére betehesd az f-et, egy olyan morfizmust kell építened, amely az I-ből csinál egy I×A inputot a formula számára. Ezt csinálja a ⟨idI​,f⟩ párképzés:

    Az első komponense az idI​: "A meglévő kontextust (a korábbi változókat) hagyd békén, passzold tovább változatlanul."

    A második komponense az f: "Az új, A típusú változó helyére pedig számold ki és tedd be az f termet."
  *)
  Let subst_f : hom I (prod I A) := ⟨id I, f⟩.

  Lemma semantic_All_Elim :
    (* Premissza: G ⊢ Π_{π₁} Φ *)
    hom G (Π_(π₁) Phi) ->

    (* Konklúzió: G ⊢ Φ[f/A] *)
    (* A Coq most már garantáltan (fobj (Pullback subst_f) Phi)-ként értelmezi! *)
    hom G (subst_f ^* Phi).
  Proof. Admitted.
End Quantifier_Elimination_Explanation.--- FILE: skeleton/Semantics/Simple/Base_Simple_Completeness.v ---

Require Import Coq.Classes.Morphisms.

Require Import HyperLogic.Cat.Core.
Require Import HyperLogic.Cat.CC.
Require Import HyperLogic.Cat.Fibration.

Require Import HyperLogic.Logic.Base.Simple.Base_Syntax.
Require Import HyperLogic.Semantics.Simple.Base_Simple_Soundness.
